## 1. Requirement map

**VERIFIED** credits evidence at the requirement’s boundary. **IMPLEMENTED_NOT_VERIFIED** means the path is wired and deciding proof is owed. **PARTIAL** means a requirement has an unresolved behavior gap or only a subset of the required outcome is evidenced. **BLOCKED** is reserved for a current lane holding the dependency. The last column records one next owner or disposition for every open row. Historical evidence within a row records what earlier runs observed. **OWNER-ATTENDED** requires a human decision or interaction. **RELEASE-CHECK** belongs to release verification. **SUPERSEDED** follows an approved decision.

### P1 — Targets, engines and operations

Basis: P1 r4d including 11-20 managed MySQL and PROGRAM D22/D26, and current shared source read at HEAD `0ce9560ea73` with concurrent changes. The original audit used retained terminal logs and source. Round 2 adds worker evidence and a pinned fixture preflight as linked per row. In both tables, `WB/` means `packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/`, `DB/` means `packages/databases/src/`, `Core/` means `packages/tests/suites/core-layer/`, `UI/` means `apps/ui/sources/`, and `E/` means this program's `evidence/`. Unit and acceptance rows concern gates and are counted separately from their underlying required capabilities.

## 3. Agent parity

The generic Session MCP Action executor reaches the declared agent surface of the qualified Actions below when the installed contribution, Session settings, caller and target admit it. Client Actions need the answering client and exact bound mount. The term **GAP** identifies an exact interaction without Action reach. Display-only gestures and choosing effect operands are called out where they differ from agent-callable reads and effects. [Action bridge](../../../../apps/cli/src/agent/tools/happierTools/createActionToolExecutorBridge.ts#L375) · [Session inventory](../../../../apps/cli/src/mcp/server/sessionMcpToolInventory.ts#L36) · [Contributed declarations](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/manifest.ts#L107)

IDs below use the canonical Database and Telemetry definitions. The November 23 census is historical. Connect, Forget, Open, addressed Navigate/Draft Read, Structure migration, Environment Clear and local observations have named IDs and present callers. [Protocol ID owner](../../../../packages/protocol/src/actions/actionIdsDatabaseV1.ts) · [November 23 census](../evidence/DBW-COMPLETENESS-1123/actions.json)

| User intent | Agent Action ID or GAP | Session MCP reach and current UI binding |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Discover checkout targets, refresh or retry, read connection identity | `happier.database/actions/targets/list`, `happier.database/actions/describe` | Generic. Checkout lists through an Action. Workbench consumes the subscribed descriptor when held and uses the Action on a miss. Discovery alone conveys no database read grant. [Checkout panel](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/DatabasesPanel.tsx#L103) · [Descriptor client](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/hostClient.ts#L156) |
| Connect/retry a saved login, forget a manual association | `happier.database/actions/targets/connect`, `happier.database/actions/targets/forget` | Generic under caller policy and installation/Account scope. Checkout now mounts both controls. The Saved Secret picker is a client operand selector, with references used at the actual effect. [Checkout controls](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/DatabasesPanel.tsx#L174) · [Connection review](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/DatabaseConnection.tsx#L25) · [Manifest](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/manifest.ts#L108) Both mounted placements now use host Action.Control for Connect and Forget. [P1/P2 lane](../lanes/DBW-COMPLETE-P1P2-1125.md). Round 2 repeated both placements and proved a forgotten target refuses before secret acquisition. [Mounted controls](../../../tmp/DBW-COMPLETE-P1P2-R2-1126/retention.log) · [Association owner](../../../tmp/DBW-COMPLETE-P1P2-R2-1126/manual-green.log) |
| Open Workbench from checkout, Environment, direct/cold location | `happier.database/actions/ui/open` | Generic + Client. Checkout and Environment call the shared opener as local navigation, bypassing the Action executor for that UI transition while sharing its destination owner. Environment supplies the resolved target from the deployment data owner. [Shared opener and registration](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/openWorkbench.ts#L105) · [Environment destination](../../../../apps/ui/sources/components/projects/deployments/DeploymentDatabasePage.tsx#L387) The Environment Database route supplies the exact selected descriptor from the existing selected Database read. Detached cleanup uses the same Environment acquisition and leaves Workbench closed. [Round 2 worker](../../../tmp/DBW-COMPLETE-P1P2-R2-1126/detached.log) |
| Switch Workbench tabs or tables, Back, FK jumps | `happier.database/actions/ui/navigate`, `happier.database/actions/structure/read`, `happier.database/actions/rows/read` | Generic + Client for Navigate, Generic for data. Native controls update local location through the same instance. The Action demands a unique current mount. The page publishes the mount address in current UI context, whose core Action is `ui.current_context.read` (binding `read_current_ui_context`). [Mount publication](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/WorkbenchPage.tsx#L182) · [Exact address dispatch](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/openWorkbench.ts#L87) · [Current UI context spec](../../../../packages/protocol/src/actions/specs/uiContext.ts#L143) |
| Browse schemas and searchable tables | `happier.database/actions/schemas/list`, `happier.database/actions/relations/list` | Generic. Search/expand are local inputs to the typed collection reads. [Action-backed provider](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/workbench.ts#L69) |
| Console Run, run selection, Explain and read-only Analyze | `happier.database/actions/statement/run`, `happier.database/actions/statement/explain` | Generic. User-selected SQL, parameters and mode go through the admitted Action. Analyze passes read mode. Agent mode and database grants decide independently of Action visibility. [Console](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/DatabaseConsole.tsx#L255) · [F10 MCP test cases](../../../../packages/tests/suites/core-layer/databaseWorkbench.agent.F10.composed.test.ts#L46) |
| Stop the admitted Console operation | `action.operations.cancel` for an accepted operation receipt; the Console aborts its outstanding `happier.database/actions/statement/run` or `happier.database/actions/statement/explain` request | The Host binding is `action_operations_cancel`. Cancellation depends on the receipt and request authority. The UI cancellation is its own request's AbortSignal. [Console controller](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/console.ts#L42) · [Operation cancellation](../../../../packages/protocol/src/actions/specs/actionOperations.ts#L199) |
| Compose SQL/parameters, selection, completions, history recall, clear history; read addressed draft/history | `happier.database/actions/ui/draft/read`, `happier.database/actions/ui/history/clear`. **SUPERSEDED** for writing the SQL draft. | Generic + Client. The Actions share the selected-database read admission under the requester and exact Workbench mount. History Clear asks by default. History Clear landed in `6562aa73703`. The bundled declaration matches source and the mounted regression passed exact History clearing, draft preservation and typed None refusal. [RED](../../../tmp/DBW-MANAGED-ACCESS-1127/history-red-worker.log) · [GREEN](../../../tmp/DBW-MANAGED-ACCESS-1127/history-green-local-retry.log). Agents compose operands for the admitted statement Action. [Mounted Actions](../../../../packages/plugins/database-workbench/src/ui/surface/openWorkbench.ts#L115) · [Draft decision](../P3-access-writes-agents.md#owner-amendment-2026-11-27-1053z-agent-draft-parity) · [November 27 lane](../lanes/DBW-MANAGED-ACCESS-1127.md) Round 2 verified Git ancestry for History Clear on the candidate Home revision. [Managed access R2](../lanes/DBW-MANAGED-ACCESS-R2-1127.md) R2 real E2EE Session MCP read the exact live mount from the answering App. History Clear on it refused with contributed_action_unavailable / current_ui_action_unavailable. ## 5. Settings misuse

The constitution reserves settings for deliberate preferences and requires key-scoped subscriptions. The table distinguishes DBW-introduced or DBW-touched setting writers from existing setting inputs and separate data owners. Writer assertions are based on production call paths.

Use 0 for manual refresh. | Settings → Plugins → Database Workbench → Activity and `plugins.settings.set` use the existing scoped plugin setting. S2 removed the previous Account key `databaseActivityAutoRefreshIntervalMs` and its bespoke writer. Worker cadence, search and Action write passed. The migrated setting awaits the carrying browser replay. [Settings S2](../lanes/DBW-SETTINGS-S2-1127.md) |
